# kwa mkono bila kuniambia. Mistari:
#   ZIP | chanzo | ndani ya zip | baada   - kinaingia kwenye zip; "baada" = GITRM:V3 (git rm, commit ni yako) | FUTA | FOLDA
#                                          (kinaondoka pamoja na folda yake kwenye FUTA_FOLDA) | BAKI
#   FUTA | njia | git | sababu             - hakiingii zip (takataka au siri isiyohitajika)
#   FUTA_FOLDA | folda | sababu            - folda nzima inaondolewa BAADA ya zip (faili zake za thamani ziko ZIP juu)
#   WORKTREE | repo | njia | branch | sha | SAFI au DIFF:<ndani ya zip>
#   TAG | repo | tag | sha  ·  TAG_PUSH | repo | tag  ·  BRANCH | repo | branch | sha | MERGED au TAG:<tag>
#   ORIGIN | repo | branch | sha | MERGED au TAG:<tag>   (git push origin --delete)
#   FICHA_ROOT | faili - default ya zamani ya root inasomwa humo na kufichwa (********) kwenye kila faili la zip
#   FICHA_FUNGUO | jina - thamani ya "jina:" kwenye kila faili la zip inafichwa (***)
